build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a map-reduce strategy:\n    1. Phase 1 (Parallel): Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Phase 2 (Sequential): Merge vocabulary and compute document frequencies\n    3. Phase 3 (Sequential): Compute IDF scores (fast, O(vocabulary_size))\n    4. Phase 4 (Parallel): Compute TF-IDF scores and document norms\n    5. Phase 5 (Sequential): Build inverted index from results\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n\n    # Create chunks for parallel processing\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, num_docs, chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # =========================================================================\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results_list = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Flatten the chunk results\n    all_results = []\n    for chunk_results in chunk_results_list:\n        all_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Merge vocabulary and compute document frequencies\n    # =========================================================================\n    vocabulary = set()\n    doc_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    doc_tf = {}     # doc_id -> term frequency dict\n\n    for result in all_results:\n        vocabulary.update(result['terms'])\n        doc_terms[result['doc_id']] = result['terms']\n        doc_tf[result['doc_id']] = result['tf']\n\n    # Calculate document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for terms in doc_terms.values() if term in terms)\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    # =========================================================================\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel TF-IDF computation and norm calculation\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Prepare args for parallel processing\n    phase4_args = [(chunk_results, idf) for chunk_results in chunk_results_list]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_chunk_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_chunk, phase4_args)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    all_tfidf_results = []\n    for chunk in tfidf_chunk_results:\n        all_tfidf_results.extend(chunk)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 5: Build inverted index and final index structure\n    # =========================================================================\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n    index.idf = idf\n\n    # Build document vectors and norms from parallel results\n    for result in all_tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors[result['doc_id']] = result['vector']\n        index.doc_norms[result['doc_id']] = result['norm']\n\n    # Build inverted index\n    inverted_index = {term: [] for term in vocabulary}\n    for result in all_tfidf_results:\n        doc_id = result['doc_id']\n        for term, score in result['vector'].items():\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, score))\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending) for efficient top-k retrieval\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    index.inverted_index = inverted_index\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker initialization\n_search_index = None\n_search_documents = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index: TFIDFIndex, documents: list[Document]):\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize worker process with search index and documents.\n\n    This is called once per worker process to avoid serializing\n    the large index repeatedly.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_documents\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_documents = documents\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for terms in doc_terms.values() if term in terms)\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    # =========================================================================\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nParallelization strategy:\n- Index building: Single-pass parallel processing with consolidated worker\n- Search: Query-level parallelism with ============================================================================\n# Data Structures for Parallel Results\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Inline tokenization functions to avoid import overhead in workers\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Regex pattern compiled once\n_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n_STOP_WORDS = frozenset([\n    \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"are\", \"as\", \"at\", \"be\", \"by\", \"for\", \"from\", \"has\", \"he\",\n    \"in\", \"is\", \"it\", \"its\", \"of\", \"on\", \"that\", \"the\", \"to\", \"was\", \"were\",\n    \"will\", \"with\", \"the\", \"this\", \"but\", \"they\", \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\",\n    \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\", \"how\", \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\",\n    \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\", \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\",\n    \"than\", \"too\", \"very\", \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\",\n    \"because\", \"about\", \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\",\n    \"below\", \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\",\n    \"any\", \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n    \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n])\n\n\ndef _tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = _TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in _STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef _compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk_full(chunk_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents completely in worker.\n\n    This single-pass approach does tokenization, TF computation,\n    and prepares everything needed for merging.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (list of (doc_id, title, content))\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_id->tf mapping, and local vocabulary\n    \"\"\"\n    documents_data = chunk_data\n\n    results = {}\n    local_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in documents_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = _tokenize(text)\n        tf = _compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        results[doc_id] = {\n            'tf': tf,\n            'terms': frozenset(tf.keys())\n        }\n        local_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return {\n        'doc_results': results,\n        'vocabulary': local_vocab\n    }\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Optimized single-pool strategy:\n    1. Phase 1 (Parallel): Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Phase 2 (Sequential): Merge results, compute DF/IDF, build index\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare lightweight document data (avoid serializing full Document objects)\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create chunks for parallel processing\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, num_docs, chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(doc_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # =========================================================================\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk_full, chunks)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Merge and compute IDF, TF-IDF, build index\n    # =========================================================================\n\n    # Merge vocabulary and document term frequencies\n    vocabulary = set()\n    all_doc_results = {}  # doc_id -> {tf, terms}\n\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        vocabulary.update(result['vocabulary'])\n        all_doc_results.update(result['doc_results'])\n\n    # Calculate document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items() if term in data['terms'])\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Compute IDF scores\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # Build document vectors, norms, and inverted index in single pass\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items():\n        tf = data['tf']\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf_val in tf.items():\n            tfidf = tf_val * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending)\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    # Build final index\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n    index.inverted_index = dict(inverted_index)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker initialization\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index: TFIDFIndex, doc_titles: dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize worker process with search index and document titles.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using the pre-initialized index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (list of query strings, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries, top_k = args\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries:\n        result = _search_single(query, top_k)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, top_k: int) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    index = _search_index\n    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = _tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = _compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                         for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses query batching to reduce per-query IPC overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document titles dict\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create query batches for workers\n    num_queries = len(queries)\n    batch_size = max(1, num_queries // num_workers)\n\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, num_queries, batch_size):\n        batch = queries[i:i + batch_size]\n        query_batches.append((batch, top_k))\n\n    # 537→"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1aa52fa7-3812-46b5-be21-68e69ee30739","uuid":"e4590b6c-662f-4394-ae22-5f0e518be102","tool_use_result":{"type":"update","filePath":"/root/workspace/parallel_solution.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides a parallel implementation of:\n1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nParallelization strategy:\n- Index building: Single-pass parallel processing with consolidated worker\n- Search: Query-level parallelism with shared index via initializer\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures for Parallel Results\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Inline tokenization functions to avoid import overhead in workers\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Regex pattern compiled once\n_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n_STOP_WORDS = frozenset([\n    \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"are\", \"as\", \"at\", \"be\", \"by\", \"for\", \"from\", \"has\", \"he\",\n    \"in\", \"is\", \"it\", \"its\", \"of\", \"on\", \"that\", \"the\", \"to\", \"was\", \"were\",\n    \"will\", \"with\", \"the\", \"this\", \"but\", \"they\", \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\",\n    \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\", \"how\", \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\",\n    \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\", \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\",\n    \"than\", \"too\", \"very\", \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\",\n    \"because\", \"about\", \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\",\n    \"below\", \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\",\n    \"any\", \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n    \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n])\n\n\ndef _tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = _TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in _STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef _compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk_full(chunk_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents completely in worker.\n\n    This single-pass approach does tokenization, TF computation,\n    and prepares everything needed for merging.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (list of (doc_id, title, content))\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_id->tf mapping, and local vocabulary\n    \"\"\"\n    documents_data = chunk_data\n\n    results = {}\n    local_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in documents_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = _tokenize(text)\n        tf = _compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        results[doc_id] = {\n            'tf': tf,\n            'terms': frozenset(tf.keys())\n        }\n        local_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return {\n        'doc_results': results,\n        'vocabulary': local_vocab\n    }\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Optimized single-pool strategy:\n    1. Phase 1 (Parallel): Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Phase 2 (Sequential): Merge results, compute DF/IDF, build index\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare lightweight document data (avoid serializing full Document objects)\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create chunks for parallel processing\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, num_docs, chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(doc_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # =========================================================================\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk_full, chunks)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Merge and compute IDF, TF-IDF, build index\n    # =========================================================================\n\n    # Merge vocabulary and document term frequencies\n    vocabulary = set()\n    all_doc_results = {}  # doc_id -> {tf, terms}\n\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        vocabulary.update(result['vocabulary'])\n        all_doc_results.update(result['doc_results'])\n\n    # Calculate document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items() if term in data['terms'])\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Compute IDF scores\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # Build document vectors, norms, and inverted index in single pass\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items():\n        tf = data['tf']\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf_val in tf.items():\n            tfidf = tf_val * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending)\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    # Build final index\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n    index.inverted_index = dict(inverted_index)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker initialization\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index: TFIDFIndex, doc_titles: dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize worker process with search index and document titles.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using the pre-initialized index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (list of query strings, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries, top_k = args\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries:\n        result = _search_single(query, top_k)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, top_k: int) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    index = _search_index\n    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = _tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = _compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                         for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses query batching to reduce per-query IPC overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document titles dict\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create query batches for workers\n    num_queries = len(queries)\n    batch_size = max(1, num_queries // num_workers)\n\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, num_queries, batch_size):\n        batch = queries[i:i + batch_size]\n        query_batches.append((batch, top_k))\n\n    # Use pool with initializer\n    with Pool(\n        num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # Test index building\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    print(\"INDEX BUILDING COMPARISON\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential\n    print(\"\\nSequential index building...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"  Time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"  Vocabulary: {seq_result.vocabulary_size} terms\")\n\n    # Parallel\n    print(f\"\\nParallel index building ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"  Time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"  Vocabulary: {par_result.vocabulary_size} terms\")\n\n    speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"\\n  Speedup: {speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\nVerifying index correctness...\")\n    seq_idx = seq_result.index\n    par_idx = par_result.index\n\n    assert seq_idx.num_documents == par_idx.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.vocabulary == par_idx.vocabulary, \"Vocabulary mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.document_frequencies == par_idx.document_frequencies, \"DF mismatch\"\n\n    # Parallel\n    print(f\"\\nParallel batch search ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        queries, par_result.index, args.top_k, args.num_workers, documents\n    )\n    print(f\"  Time: {par_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    search_speedup = seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"\\n  Speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nParallelization strategy:\n- Index building: Map-Reduce pattern with chunked document processing\n- Search: Query-level parallelism with shared index via initializer\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures for Parallel Results\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(documents: list[Document]) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n\n    This is Phase 1 of the map-reduce pipeline.\n    Each worker processes multiple documents to reduce IPC overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to process\n\n    Returns:\n        List of dicts containing doc_id, term frequencies, and term sets\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append({\n            'doc_id': doc.doc_id,\n            'tf': tf,\n            'terms': set(tf.keys())\n        })\n    return results\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_for_chunk(args: tuple) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute TF-IDF vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is Phase 3 of the map-reduce pipeline.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk_results, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of dicts containing doc_id, tfidf vector, and norm\n    \"\"\"\n    chunk_results, idf = args\n    tfidf_results = []\n\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        doc_id = result['doc_id']\n        tf = result['tf']\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf_val in tf.items():\n            tfidf = tf_val * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        tfidf_results.append({\n            'doc_id': doc_id,\n            'vector': doc_vector,\n            'norm': math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n        })\n\n    return tfidf_results\n\n\n# build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Uses a map-reduce strategy:\n    1. Phase 1 (Parallel): Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Phase 2 (Sequential): Merge vocabulary and compute document frequencies\n    3. Phase 3 (Sequential): Compute IDF scores (fast, O(vocabulary_size))\n    4. Phase 4 (Parallel): Compute TF-IDF scores and document norms\n    5. Phase 5 (Sequential): Build inverted index from results\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n\n    # Create chunks for parallel processing\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, num_docs, chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(documents[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # =========================================================================\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results_list = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Flatten the chunk results\n    all_results = []\n    for chunk_results in chunk_results_list:\n        all_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Merge vocabulary and compute document frequencies\n    # =========================================================================\n    vocabulary = set()\n    doc_terms = {}  # doc_id -> set of terms\n    doc_tf = {}     # doc_id -> term frequency dict\n\n    for result in all_results:\n        vocabulary.update(result['terms'])\n        doc_terms[result['doc_id']] = result['terms']\n        doc_tf[result['doc_id']] = result['tf']\n\n    # Calculate document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for terms in doc_terms.values() if term in terms)\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    # =========================================================================\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 4: Parallel TF-IDF computation and norm calculation\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Prepare args for parallel processing\n    phase4_args = [(chunk_results, idf) for chunk_results in chunk_results_list]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        tfidf_chunk_results = pool.map(_compute_tfidf_for_chunk, phase4_args)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    all_tfidf_results = []\n    for chunk in tfidf_chunk_results:\n        all_tfidf_results.extend(chunk)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 5: Build inverted index and final index structure\n    # =========================================================================\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n    index.idf = idf\n\n    # Build document vectors and norms from parallel results\n    for result in all_tfidf_results:\n        index.doc_vectors[result['doc_id']] = result['vector']\n        index.doc_norms[result['doc_id']] = result['norm']\n\n    # Build inverted index\n    inverted_index = {term: [] for term in vocabulary}\n    for result in all_tfidf_results:\n        doc_id = result['doc_id']\n        for term, score in result['vector'].items():\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, score))\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending) for efficient top-k retrieval\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    index.inverted_index = inverted_index\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker initialization\n_search_index = None\n_search_documents = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index: TFIDFIndex, documents: list[Document]):\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize worker process with search index and documents.\n\n    This is called once per worker process to avoid serializing\n    the large index repeatedly.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_documents\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_documents = documents\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using the pre-initialized index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query_string, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, top_k = args\n    return search_sequential(query, _search_index, top_k, _search_documents)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Each query is processed independently by a worker process.\n    The index is shared via initializer to avoid repeated serialization.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare query arguments\n    query_args = [(query, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    # Use pool with initializer to share index efficiently\n    with Pool(\n        num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, documents)\n    ) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, query_args)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# functions to avoid import overhead in workers\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Regex pattern compiled once\n_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n_STOP_WORDS = frozenset([\n    \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"are\", \"as\", \"at\", \"be\", \"by\", \"for\", \"from\", \"has\", \"he\",\n    \"in\", \"is\", \"it\", \"its\", \"of\", \"on\", \"that\", \"the\", \"to\", \"was\", \"were\",\n    \"will\", \"with\", \"the\", \"this\", \"but\", \"they\", \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\",\n    \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\", \"how\", \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\",\n    \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\", \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\",\n    \"than\", \"too\", \"very\", \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\",\n    \"because\", \"about\", \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\",\n    \"below\", \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\",\n    \"any\", \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n    \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n])\n\n\ndef _tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = _TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in _STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef _compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk_full(chunk_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents completely in worker.\n\n    This single-pass approach does tokenization, TF computation,\n    and prepares everything needed for merging.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (list of (doc_id, title, content))\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_id->tf mapping, and local vocabulary\n    \"\"\"\n    documents_data = chunk_data\n\n    results = {}\n    local_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in documents_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = _tokenize(text)\n        tf = _compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        results[doc_id] = {\n            'tf': tf,\n            'terms': frozenset(tf.keys())\n        }\n        local_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return {\n        'doc_results': results,\n        'vocabulary': local_vocab\n    }\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Optimized single-pool strategy:\n    1. Phase 1 (Parallel): Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Phase 2 (Sequential): Merge results, compute DF/IDF, build index\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare lightweight document data (avoid serializing full Document objects)\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create chunks for parallel processing\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, num_docs, chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(doc_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # =========================================================================\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk_full, chunks)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Merge and compute IDF, TF-IDF, build index\n    # =========================================================================\n\n    # Merge vocabulary and document term frequencies\n    vocabulary = set()\n    all_doc_results = {}  # doc_id -> {tf, terms}\n\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        vocabulary.update(result['vocabulary'])\n        all_doc_results.update(result['doc_results'])\n\n    # Calculate document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items() if term in data['terms'])\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Compute IDF scores\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # Build document vectors, norms, and inverted index in single pass\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items():\n        tf = data['tf']\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf_val in tf.items():\n            tfidf = tf_val * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending)\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    # Build final index\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n    index.inverted_index = dict(inverted_index)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker initialization\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index: TFIDFIndex, doc_titles: dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize worker process with search index and document titles.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using the pre-initialized index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (list of query strings, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries, top_k = args\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries:\n        result = _search_single(query, top_k)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, top_k: int) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    index = _search_index\n    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = _tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = _compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                         for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses query batching to reduce per-query IPC overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document titles dict\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create query batches for workers\n    num_queries = len(queries)\n    batch_size = max(1, num_queries // num_workers)\n\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, num_queries, batch_size):\n        batch = queries[i:i + batch_size]\n        query_batches.append((batch, top_k))\n\n    # Use pool with initializer\n    with Pool(\n        num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # child processes will inherit this\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n    # Create query batches for workers\n    num_queries = len(queries)\n    batch_size = max(1, num_queries // num_workers)\n\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, num_queries, batch_size):\n        batch = queries[i:i + batch_size]\n        query_batches.append((batch, top_k))\n\n    # Use fork context to share memory (avoids serializing index)\n    ctx = get_context('fork')\n    with ctx.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides a parallel implementation of:\n1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using parallel query processing\n\nParallelization strategy:\n- Index building: Single-pass parallel processing with consolidated worker\n- Search: Query-level parallelism with shared index via initializer\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, get_context\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures for Parallel Results\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Inline tokenization functions to avoid import overhead in workers\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Regex pattern compiled once\n_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n_STOP_WORDS = frozenset([\n    \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"are\", \"as\", \"at\", \"be\", \"by\", \"for\", \"from\", \"has\", \"he\",\n    \"in\", \"is\", \"it\", \"its\", \"of\", \"on\", \"that\", \"the\", \"to\", \"was\", \"were\",\n    \"will\", \"with\", \"the\", \"this\", \"but\", \"they\", \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\",\n    \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\", \"how\", \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\",\n    \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\", \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\",\n    \"than\", \"too\", \"very\", \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\",\n    \"because\", \"about\", \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\",\n    \"below\", \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\",\n    \"any\", \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n    \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n])\n\n\ndef _tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = _TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in _STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef _compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk_full(chunk_data: tuple) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents completely in worker.\n\n    This single-pass approach does tokenization, TF computation,\n    and prepares everything needed for merging.\n\n    Args:\n        chunk_data: Tuple of (list of (doc_id, title, content))\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with doc_id->tf mapping, and local vocabulary\n    \"\"\"\n    documents_data = chunk_data\n\n    results = {}\n    local_vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in documents_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = _tokenize(text)\n        tf = _compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        results[doc_id] = {\n            'tf': tf,\n            'terms': frozenset(tf.keys())\n        }\n        local_vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return {\n        'doc_results': results,\n        'vocabulary': local_vocab\n    }\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index using parallel processing.\n\n    Optimized single-pool strategy:\n    1. Phase 1 (Parallel): Tokenize documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Phase 2 (Sequential): Merge results, compute DF/IDF, build index\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for parallel processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare lightweight document data (avoid serializing full Document objects)\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create chunks for parallel processing\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, num_docs, chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(doc_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # =========================================================================\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk_full, chunks)\n\n    # =========================================================================\n    # Phase 2: Merge and compute IDF, TF-IDF, build index\n    # =========================================================================\n\n    # Merge vocabulary and document term frequencies\n    vocabulary = set()\n    all_doc_results = {}  # doc_id -> {tf, terms}\n\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        vocabulary.update(result['vocabulary'])\n        all_doc_results.update(result['doc_results'])\n\n    # Calculate document frequencies\n    document_frequencies = {}\n    for term in vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items() if term in data['terms'])\n        document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Compute IDF scores\n    N = num_docs\n    idf = {term: math.log(N / df) + 1 for term, df in document_frequencies.items()}\n\n    # Build document vectors, norms, and inverted index in single pass\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for doc_id, data in all_doc_results.items():\n        tf = data['tf']\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf_val in tf.items():\n            tfidf = tf_val * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score (descending)\n    for term in inverted_index:\n        inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    # Build final index\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = document_frequencies\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n    index.inverted_index = dict(inverted_index)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for worker initialization\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index: TFIDFIndex, doc_titles: dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize worker process with search index and document titles.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = index\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(args: tuple) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using the pre-initialized index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (list of query strings, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    queries, top_k = args\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries:\n        result = _search_single(query, top_k)\n        results.append(result)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, top_k: int) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    index = _search_index\n    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = _tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = _compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                         for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Batch Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses query batching to reduce per-query IPC overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document titles dict\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create query batches for workers\n    num_queries = len(queries)\n    batch_size = max(1, num_queries // num_workers)\n\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, num_queries, batch_size):\n        batch = queries[i:i + batch_size]\n        query_batches.append((batch, top_k))\n\n    # Use pool with initializer\n    with Pool(\n        num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten results\n    results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Sequential\n    print(\"\\nSequential index building...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"  Time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n    print(f\"  Vocabulary: {seq_result.vocabulary_size} terms\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\nVerifying index correctness...\")\n    seq_idx = seq_result.index\n    par_idx = par_result.index\n\n    assert seq_idx.num_documents == par_idx.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.vocabulary == par_idx.vocabulary, \"Vocabulary mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.document_frequencies == par_idx.document_frequencies, \"DF mismatch\"\n\n    #
